The `PaletteListState` indirection decouples the provider from `Host` to keep the module cycle-free; `paletteListNameThunk` casts the void-pointer back to `*const Host`. `Host.buildPaletteListName` produces strings like "Command palette, 3 of 87: New Tab — new_tab" so Narrator announces the selected entry when the list is inspected. Selection-change `UIA_NamePropertyId` events need a stable provider instance to target; per-row `IRawElementProviderFragment` + `IInvokeProvider` item providers are not exposed by this provider.
- 2026-04-16: `App.init` calls `CoInitializeEx(null, COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED)` to bring the main-thread STA apartment online; `App.terminate` pairs it with `CoUninitialize` **only when the init actually succeeded** (`com_initialized` flag). `S_OK` / `S_FALSE` set the flag; `RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE` does not — another component owns the apartment and `CoUninitialize` would unbalance their ref. STA-only consumers like `IFileOpenDialog` fail predictably in the `RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE` branch. Future WinRT (toast factory), OLE (drag-drop), and Shell (`IShellLink` AUMID shortcuts) consumers all depend on this apartment. Do NOT re-call `CoInitializeEx(COINIT_MULTITHREADED)` on the main thread; spawn a worker thread for MTA work instead.
- 2026-04-16: `src/apprt/win32_settings.zig` owns the native settings window (`noctty.win32.settings` WNDCLASS, top-level HWND, singleton on `App`). `AppHandle` is a minimal callback surface (`ctx`, `alloc`, `hinstance`, `chromeBg`, `textPrimary`, `openInEditor`, `currentConfig`, `saveAndReload`) so the settings module never pulls `Host` / `Surface` / the palette into its dep graph. Action rerouting: `open_config` calls `App.settings_window.open`; `App.openConfigInEditor` backs the "Open in default editor" button in the Advanced section. `SettingsWindow` is idempotent: repeated `open` calls raise an existing window rather than spawning another instance. Pending edits live on a `Config.shallowClone(handle.alloc)` draft (inheriting the `command` override without re-deiniting it per AGENTS.md:49); `save` uses the app settings-save path and re-reads through `configpkg.Config.load → core_app.updateConfig`. The in-window controls are data-driven from the catalogue in `field_catalogue.zig` — one entry per editable key, dispatched by control kind (bool → checkbox, int / float → spinner, enum → combo, string → edit).
- 2026-04-16: Palette list mouse-wheel scroll (`Host.scrollPaletteList`) MUST clamp `palette_list_selected` into `[new_scroll, new_scroll + palette_max_visible_rows)` after updating the scroll position. Without the clamp, Enter invokes whatever row the selection pointed at _before_ the scroll — an off-screen action the user has no visual cue about. This also keeps UIA Name ("Command palette, N of M") in sync with what the user sees.
- 2026-04-16: `src/shell-integration/powershell/integration.ps1` derives OSC 133 D exit status from `$?` first, then `$LASTEXITCODE`. PowerShell only updates `$LASTEXITCODE` for native executables; cmdlet / script / `throw` failures leave it stale while flipping `$?` to `$false`. Reading `$?` first surfaces those failures as non-zero (synthesising `1` if `$LASTEXITCODE` is null or zero), while preserving real native exit codes when `$?` is `$true`. Do not collapse back to bare `$LASTEXITCODE` — jump-to-prompt accuracy depends on the composite.
- 2026-04-16: Settings window native control catalogue ships 11 editable fields — Terminal: `scrollback-limit`, `confirm-close-surface`, `copy-on-select`, `clipboard-trim-trailing-spaces`; Appearance: `font-size`, `background-opacity`, `window-theme`, `cursor-style`, `window-padding-balance`, `background-blur` (boolean projection of the three-variant union — checkbox only controls the false/true path, a numeric radius set via the config file survives a save only if the user leaves the checkbox in its auto-derived state); Shell: `shell-integration`. Each field is a handwritten sync helper pair (`syncXFromY` / `displayXInY`) pointing at the `pending` clone — no comptime catalogue reflection yet (the custom types `WindowPadding`, `BellFeatures`, `RepeatableString`, optional `command` don't fit a generic editor and stay in the Advanced escape-hatch lane). `suppress_edit_events` guards the display-helpers' programmatic `SendMessageW(WM_SETTEXT / BM_SETCHECK / CB_SETCURSEL)` from reentering the sync-helpers via `EN_CHANGE` / `BN_CLICKED` / `CBN_SELCHANGE`.
- 2026-04-16: `src/apprt/win32_aumid.zig` owns AUMID registration: `SetCurrentProcessExplicitAppUserModelID(L"io.github.amanthanvi.noctty")` runs at the top of `App.init` **before** any HWND is created (taskbar identity is snapshotted at first window creation) and `HKCU\Software\Classes\AppUserModelId\io.github.amanthanvi.noctty` is populated with `DisplayName=noctty` + `ShowInSettings=1`. Both calls log-and-continue on failure — toast attribution and taskbar grouping are non-essential. Our AUMID `io.github.amanthanvi.noctty` uses our own namespace (matching the instance/bundle id), so it cannot collide with — or imply affiliation with — an upstream Ghostty install. Icon URI + Start Menu shortcut writing live in the installer (`scripts/package-windows.ps1`), not the runtime.
- 2026-04-16: `Shell.powershell` is a first-class variant in `src/termio/shell_integration.zig`; `detectShell` matches `pwsh` / `pwsh.exe` / `powershell` / `powershell.exe` case-insensitively. Automatic injection now wraps interactive launches only, preserving prefix flags and skipping explicit `-Command` / `-File`-style entrypoints so exit behavior and payload semantics stay intact. Manual `$PROFILE` sourcing of `integration.ps1` remains the fallback path.
- 2026-04-16: `App.os_build` caches the real kernel build number from `RtlGetVersion` (probed once in `App.init`); `App.use_integrated_titlebar = os_build >= 22000`. Keep this as the sole platform gate for integrated titlebar, Snap Layouts, and Mica-Tabbed behavior. Do not expose a config knob for this (platform-automatic per §12 Q1). `GetVersionExW` is banned because Win10+ shims lie about the build number unless the exe carries a Win10 app-manifest GUID; `RtlGetVersion` is unshimmed.
- 2026-04-16: `App.is_live_resize: std.atomic.Value(bool)` tracks the `WM_ENTERSIZEMOVE` / `WM_EXITSIZEMOVE` window. `Surface.requestRepaint` loads it and short-circuits renderer-thread repaint wake-ups during drag — the OS's own WM_SIZE → WM_PAINT cascade covers pacing, so stacking extra `InvalidateRect` calls from the renderer wake path just wastes pump work. `WM_EXITSIZEMOVE` fires one guaranteed final `InvalidateRect(null, 0)` so no frame is permanently lost, and the flag is defensively cleared on `WM_SIZE` with `SIZE_MAXIMIZED` / `SIZE_MINIMIZED` wParams because those jumps can skip the modal-loop `EXITSIZEMOVE`. The flag is atomic because the renderer thread reads it from outside the UI pump.
- 2026-04-16: `src/apprt/win32_clipboard_html.zig` is the CF_HTML serialiser. `wrapFragment` produces the full `Version:0.9` + zero-padded-10-digit-offset header + `<html><body>` + `<!--StartFragment-->`...`<!--EndFragment-->` frame. Offsets are **load-bearing**: Word / WordPad / OneNote parse by fixed-width byte positions; never "optimise" the `{d:0>10}` padding away. `Surface.writeClipboardHtml` registers the "HTML Format" clipboard format once per `App` (cached as `App.cf_html_format`), writes both CF_HTML and CF_UNICODETEXT in a single `OpenClipboard` / `EmptyClipboard` / `Close` cycle, and uses `GlobalAlloc(GMEM_MOVEABLE | GMEM_ZEROINIT)` — the clipboard owns the handle on success so `errdefer GlobalFree` is only unwound on failure.
- 2026-04-16: `src/apprt/win32_undo.zig` is the per-surface bounded undo stack (16 entries, 8 MB aggregate; oversized single entry accepted at the cost of evicting everything else). Owned by `Surface.undo_stack`, drained by `Surface.destroy` → `undo_stack.deinit` which walks every snapshot variant and frees its owned byte slices. Win32 now uses it for terminal-local `clear_screen` / `reset` undo+redo; host-scoped `close_tab:this` and `new_split` replay live on `Host.structural_*`, and unsupported structural mutations must explicitly invalidate history instead of pretending to be recoverable.
- 2026-04-16: `applyDwmThemeWithBuild` is the build-number-aware DWM theme path. Main-window backdrop selection: build ≥ 22621 → `DWMSBT_TABBEDWINDOW` (Mica-with-tabs); build < 22621 → skip `DWMWA_SYSTEMBACKDROP_TYPE` entirely. The build gate prevents `E_INVALIDARG` log-spam from probing unsupported system-backdrop attributes on Windows 10 and Win11 21H2. Call sites with `App` in scope should pass `self.os_build`; do not add a build-unaware wrapper unless a real caller needs Win10 fallback semantics.
- 2026-04-17: `HostOverlayMode.confirm` is the canonical non-modal confirmation path, replacing the `MessageBoxW` stopgap. `Host.showConfirm(title, body, accept_label, cancel_label, on_accept, on_cancel, userdata)` deep-copies every string (Host owns them until the overlay closes) and is mutually exclusive with palette / profile / search — a new `showConfirm` calls `hideOverlay` on the previous mode first. Accept (Enter / button 2003) → `invokeConfirmAccept` calls `hideOverlay` + `layout` FIRST, then dispatches the callback (see AGENTS.md:89 — "snapshot + clear BEFORE dispatch" is load-bearing, a destructive accept may free the Host); Cancel (Esc / button 2004) → `invokeConfirmCancel` same ordering. `Surface.close(process_active=true)` routes through this (no more modal pump-blocking); a `@constCast` on the Surface is required because core's close contract hands us `*const Surface` while the callback pointer needs a mutable address for later `DestroyWindow`. If a Host isn't available (shouldn't happen in practice, but pre-init close paths exist), `Surface.close` falls through to direct `DestroyWindow` and logs — preserves the "config silently ignored" fix even if the overlay path is unreachable. `MessageBoxW` is now banned in the Win32 apprt except for the pre-init fatal-error dialog.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_link_preview.zig` is an allocation-free hover-dwell state machine. `HoverTracker.onMouseMove` + `tick` emit `{none, show, hide}` actions the caller routes to a tooltip HWND. Dwell threshold = 500 ms; movement beyond 3 px _on the same link_ resets the dwell timer (not cancel) so users adjusting their cursor over a long URL don't drop back to idle. `shown` times out at 5 s even if the cursor hasn't moved. `dismiss()` from any non-idle state returns `.hide`. Link strings are borrowed — the tracker never allocates.
- 2026-04-17: `settingsSaveAndReload` writes against the resolved target config file, never the merged runtime config. It builds `Config.default(alloc)` + `loadFile(target_path)`, copies GUI-edited value-type fields from `pending`, and lets `patchOrAppendEdits` update the raw target text so comments, formatting, relative `config-file` paths, and unrelated fields are preserved. Pointer fields (strings, slices) need an explicit arena-transfer branch if ever exposed via the GUI catalogue.
- 2026-04-17: `Shell.powershell` setup in `src/termio/shell_integration.zig` now returns a real direct argv for supported interactive launches and returns `null` only for unsupported modes (explicit command/script/non-interactive entrypoints, missing script resource, non-Windows). `termio/Exec.zig` logs the generic "automatic shell integration not injected" path for these intentional skips instead of claiming shell detection failed.
- 2026-04-17: `Host.invokeConfirmAccept` / `invokeConfirmCancel` MUST snapshot the callback + userdata locally, call `hideOverlay` + `layout` (which frees the payload), and only THEN dispatch the callback. A destructive accept (e.g. confirm-close-surface) synchronously destroys the Host via `DestroyWindow`, so any `self.*` access after the callback is a use-after-free. Compare with `invokePaletteRow` which has the same "do not access `self` after dispatch" constraint. **Call sites** (host wndproc button 2003/2004, overlayEditProc VK_ESCAPE, submitOverlay `.confirm`) must also return immediately — NO `refocusActiveSurface(v)` / `layout()` after the call, the Host pointer may already be freed. `invokeConfirmAccept` handles focus restoration itself where safe (via the callback's own teardown path); the overlay-close path does not need to call `refocusActiveSurface`.
- 2026-04-17: `HostOverlayMode.confirm` is a message-only overlay: `showOverlay` hides the EDIT for this mode (there's no query to type) and focuses the Accept button on show. The payload `body` is routed to the hint HWND via `syncOverlayHint`'s `.confirm` early-branch — the generic `buildOverlayHintText` returns `""` for `.confirm` so the hint stays unused in the fallback path. Default-focus on the Accept button is safe because Enter = Accept and Esc = Cancel are already wired; initial focus is one explicit press away from either gesture. Never default-focus the EDIT in this mode — the edit is hidden, and directing keyboard input to an invisible control is a UX trap.
- 2026-04-17: Live-resize state lives on `Host.is_live_resize`, NOT `App`. Dragging window A must not freeze renderer invalidations for background windows B / C — an App-wide flag caused cross-window resize stalls. `Surface.requestRepaint` gates on `self.host.?.is_live_resize` when a host is attached; surfaces without a host (pre-init, quick-terminal create) fall through without gating. `WM_ENTERSIZEMOVE` / `WM_EXITSIZEMOVE` / defensive `SIZE_MAXIMIZED|MINIMIZED` clear all run on the Host's flag.
- 2026-04-17: Settings `AppHandle.saveAndReload` takes BOTH `pending` and `original`. The save path diffs against `original` (the snapshot captured when the settings window opened), NEVER against `App.config`. Without this, a concurrent `reload_config` or external file edit while the window is open would cause our save to silently revert the newer values for every GUI-exposed field whose stale draft differs from the refreshed runtime state. `SettingsWindow.original` is an owned `Config` via `shallowClone` (not a pointer to live `App.config`); `clearPending` deinits both original and pending. `adoptCurrentConfig` makes two independent shallow clones so each struct has its own arena.
- 2026-04-17: `SurfaceInitOptions.passive_show` controls the Host's initial `ShowWindow` flag: `false` (default) → `SW_SHOW` (activates + brings to foreground); `true` → `SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE` (visible but does not steal focus). Used by `quick-terminal-keyboard-interactivity = none` on first toggle-on. Skipping `surface.present()` alone is insufficient because `createHost` unconditionally called `SW_SHOW` before this was plumbed through — the passive flag is the gate at window creation time.
- 2026-04-18: After `19f09c1bf` ("Fix PowerShell launch and auto-injection on Windows"), bare `powershell.exe` works again under sandboxed Win32 `-e` probes; the old `GetLastError(2)` launch workaround is obsolete. For deterministic scrollbar validation, `cmd.exe /k ...` still produces more reliable bulk scrollback than PowerShell `-File` probes.
- 2026-04-18: For layered Win32 scrollbar validation, screen-capturing the scrollbar rect can show the underlying terminal content and produce false zero-diff results even when the HWND is alive. Verify hover/show via `GetLayeredWindowAttributes` and verify drag via real scrollbar state changes (or logs), not screen diffs alone.
- 2026-04-18: Win32 overlay scrollbars that use a layered child HWND must never auto-hide to global alpha `0` if hover wake-up depends on real mouse input. An alpha-zero layered child stops receiving hover hit-tests, so the scrollbar can strand itself hidden; keep a non-zero alpha floor for the hidden-but-hoverable state.
- 2026-04-18: The interactive Win11 harness boots under Windows PowerShell 5.1. Temp validation scripts must avoid PS7-only syntax (for example ternary expressions and trailing commas) and avoid case-insensitive collisions with built-ins like `$PID`.
- 2026-04-18: For docked-search keyboard validation under the sandboxed Win11 harness, foreground `SendInput` is not a reliable `Esc` probe even when the search edit visibly has focus. Send `WM_KEYDOWN/WM_KEYUP` for `VK_ESCAPE` directly to the `2101` search edit HWND; that matches `searchEditProc` and avoids false "Escape close is broken" failures.
- 2026-04-18: In Win32 split-search automation, hidden `2101` search-edit HWNDs can exist for other tabs and panes with misleading geometry, and a fresh peer split may not allocate its own `2101` at all until that pane first owns docked search. To validate pane-follow behavior, try candidate hidden edits when they exist and only accept the activation path that keeps `VISIBLE_SURFACE_COUNT=2` and yields a different visible search edit; rect/width heuristics alone are not reliable.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_icons.zig` is the single source of truth for the 18 geometric icons used across palette rows, settings, search bar, toast severity glyphs, and titlebar fallback glyphs. `drawIcon(kind, hdc, rect, color, is_hc)` paints via `CreatePen` / `SelectObject` / `Ellipse` / `Rectangle` / `Polygon` / `MoveToEx` / `LineTo`. HC mode uses `NULL_BRUSH` + 1 px stroke (outline-only) for system-background visibility; normal mode uses solid fill + 2 px stroke. `regex`, `case_sens`, `whole_word` glyphs are geometric approximations (NOT `DrawTextW`) to avoid font-selection complexity. All icons include an `iconContentRect` 12% padding pass so the visible shape never touches the button edge.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_paste_protection.zig` classifies clipboard/drop payloads into a 5-level severity ladder: `safe < contains_newline < mixed_content < shell_metachar < control_chars`. `inspect()` returns the HIGHEST tier found. `hasShellMetachar` is deliberately narrower than `win32_surface_drop.requiresQuoting` — this module gates a DESTRUCTIVE CONFIRM prompt, so we only flag execution-changing chars (`;`, `|`, `&`, backtick, `$(`, `${`, `>`, `<`) and NOT quoting-nuisance chars (`*`, `?`, spaces, bare `$var`). The mixed-content heuristic uses a 6-non-whitespace-byte threshold outside URL spans (not a raw 2× length ratio) — bare URL pastes and multi-URL pastes pass; prose-embedded URLs fail. Surface drop paths call `inspect` and show `HostOverlayMode.confirm` with `verdict.reason` as body when `severity != .safe`.
- 2026-04-17: `AppHandle.notifySuccess(title, body)` gives Settings save local chrome feedback through `showHostBanner` and only escalates to system notifications when the config allows reload notifications or no host banner can render. `Surface.search_bar` (per-pane), `Host.drag_state` (per-host), `Surface.undo_stack`, `Host.tween_sched`, and `App.link_hover_tracker` are non-dead infrastructure fields tied to their module's lifetime — init on creation, deinit on destroy, ready for their respective HWND wire-ups.
- 2026-04-17: Quick terminal visibility + geometry must apply to the **host** HWND (`surface.windowHwnd()` / `host.hwnd`), NEVER to `surface.hwnd` (the child GL surface). The child is contained inside the host's client area; hiding or moving it leaves an empty top-level frame on-screen and `MonitorFromWindow` against the child resolves to the host's current monitor instead of the one the host is about to be moved to. Both `toggleQuickTerminal` reuse/create paths now route through `surface.windowHwnd()`.
- 2026-04-17: Settings save writes to the **active** config file, not always the per-user default. `cliConfigFileOverride(alloc)` scans process argv for `--config-file <path>` (and `--config-file=<path>`); when set, the save path targets that file via `std.fs.realpathAlloc`. Falls back to `config_edit.openPath` for the no-override case. If a user launched `noctty --config-file custom.conf`, saving in the settings UI updates `custom.conf`; writing to the default would either edit the wrong file or get masked on reload by the CLI-provided config.
- 2026-04-17: Settings save emit loop force-emits GUI-edited fields even when their value equals `Config.default()`. Without this, resetting a field that an included `config-file` sets to a non-default value (e.g. `window-theme = dark`) back to the stock default in the GUI would silently fail — `FileFormatter(.changed=true)` would drop the reset line and the include would re-apply its non-default value on reload. Implementation: track the GUI-edited set via `std.StaticBitSet` keyed on `Config.Key`, and in the per-field emit loop emit when `(is_user_edited or differs_from_default)`. The hand-rolled loop replaces the `FileFormatter` call for settings saves; the formatter is still used by the CLI's `+show-config` path and by doc generation.
- 2026-04-17: Settings save baseline is loaded from `target_path` via `Config.loadFile`, NOT `loadDefaultFiles`. When launched with `--config-file custom.conf`, `target_path` resolves to `custom.conf` (via `cliConfigFileOverride` → `absolutizePath`); loading the default search path would overwrite the custom file with defaults + GUI edits only, silently dropping every key that existed only in the custom file. `loadFile` tolerates a missing target (first-save case) via an `error.FileNotFound` catch → proceeds with the default-only baseline. `realpathAlloc` is banned for this specific path-resolution because it `CreateFileW`s the target — fails before the first save writes the file.
- 2026-04-17: `src/shell-integration/powershell/integration.ps1` snapshots `$LASTEXITCODE` into `$Global:__ghostty_prev_exitcode` **AFTER** `__ghostty_original_prompt` returns, not before. Git-aware prompts (the common case) run native helpers like `git` or `cmd /c` which overwrite `$LASTEXITCODE`; snapshotting pre-prompt would cause the next user-typed cmdlet to inherit the prompt-helper's exit code as its "fresh native" baseline. With the post-prompt snapshot, the user's command's `$LASTEXITCODE` delta is measured against the prompt-helper's outcome — which is the correct reference.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_scrollbar_geometry.zig` owns the graphical scrollbar's visibility state machine (`hidden → fading_in → visible → fading_out → hidden`) + track / thumb rect math. Fade-cancel preserves alpha: a scroll during fading-out switches to fading-in and back-computes `fade_started_ms` so the fade-in continues from the current alpha (no pop). `pointOverTrack` always uses the hover-expanded width for hit-testing regardless of the current `hovered` flag — prevents edge-flicker at the 1 px boundary between skinny and hovered widths. GL renderer wire-up consumes `trackRect` / `thumbRect` / `alpha` per frame.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_nc_layout.zig` computes the `WM_NCCALCSIZE` client rect + `WM_NCHITTEST` zone classification for the Win11 integrated titlebar. Maximized state adds `size_frame_y + padded_border` to the top margin to compensate for Win11's invisible on-maximize resize margin; without this, content clips into the monitor bezel. `HTMAXBUTTON` at the pixel-exact `captionButtonsRect(..).max` rect triggers Snap Layouts on Win11 22H2+ — NEVER shrink or round the rect. Sysmenu occludes the top-left corner resize zone intentionally; clicking top-left opens the system menu, matching Win32 convention.
- 2026-04-17: `quick-terminal-screen = main` uses `MonitorFromPoint({0,0}, MONITOR_DEFAULTTOPRIMARY)` — the primary monitor's work-area always contains origin per Windows docs. `MonitorFromWindow(hwnd)` returns the window's current monitor, which re-selects whatever display the QT last landed on. After the QT is ever shown on a secondary display, reopening with `main` would otherwise keep targeting that secondary display — wrong semantic.
- 2026-04-17: `QuickTerminalConfig.size_secondary: ?SizePercent` carries the second axis of `quick-terminal-size = WIDTH,HEIGHT`. Only consulted for `position = .center` — edge-docked positions fill the perpendicular axis from the work-area completely. When null, `.center` falls back to `size` on both axes (equal-square default). `sizeFromConfig` + `sizeSecondaryFromConfig` + `sizeToGeometry` in win32.zig map the core config's `Size` union onto `SizePercent`; keep mixed-unit values such as `quick-terminal-size = 50%,500px` two-dimensional on Windows.
- 2026-04-17: `settingsSaveAndReload` post-save validation: after `Config.load()` returns the refreshed config, walk the `user_edited` bitset and compare `pending.changed(&reloaded, key)` for each GUI-edited field. When any masked field is found, return `SaveError.SavedButMasked` — distinct from generic write failures. `SettingsWindow.save` catches this variant and emits a warning toast ("Settings saved — some values are masked by a later config-file layer") instead of a plain success, so users understand their edit IS on disk but the effective runtime value doesn't match.
- 2026-04-17: `cliConfigFileOverride` picks the LAST `--config-file` occurrence, NOT the first. `Config.loadRecursiveFiles` applies files in argv order and later files override earlier ones; writing to the first file when a later one also sets the same key would make the save masked-on-reload. The `while` loop advances past the path argument with `i += 1` after matching the separated form so a path that happens to start with `--config-file` doesn't get mistaken for a key.
- 2026-04-17: Settings save is surgical — `patchOrAppendEdits` reads the raw target file text, replaces only the GUI-edited key lines, and preserves everything else byte-identical: comments, blank lines, relative `config-file` paths (not canonicalised), and fields we don't round-trip through the Config type (RepeatableString et al). Line match: trim-left → key before `=`, with `#` / `;` / empty-line comments skipped. Duplicate key lines in the source are all replaced. User-edited keys not in the source are appended at the end. This supersedes the old `FileFormatter`-based full-rewrite which canonicalised relative paths on every save.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_tab_visual.zig` owns the tab hover close-button fade (`CloseState` — 120 ms cubic ease-in-out, captures current alpha on mid-fade reversal so quick hover-in/out doesn't snap) and the focused-tab underline slide (`UnderlineState` — 160 ms cubic ease-in-out between tab rects, retarget mid-slide captures current rect). Zero-state (`slide_started_ms == 0`) snaps to target without animation so the first focused tab doesn't slide in from the left on window open. `closeHitRect` expands in lockstep with alpha so pointer hit-testing matches the visually rendered button.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_focus_ring.zig` owns the `keyboard` / `mouse` mode flag driving chrome focus-ring visibility. `onKeyDown` flips to keyboard, `onMouseInput` flips to mouse; both return a bool so callers can skip a repaint when the state is unchanged. `ringRect` insets 2 px from the outer rect (1 px boundary + 1 px stroke interior); degenerate collapse kicks in when `half_width <= inset_px` to avoid invisible hairline strokes. Keep `shouldShowRing` free of size / fade gates — compose with `isDrawable` at the paint site. Wire each chrome control's WNDPROC to the tracker.
- 2026-04-17: Settings save failure preserves the pending draft — only the success + SavedButMasked paths call `clearPending` + `adoptCurrentConfig`. Real errors (`TempCreateFailed`, `ReplaceFailed`, `ReloadFailed`, `PathResolveFailed`, `SerializeFailed`, `OutOfMemory`) log a warn and leave `pending` intact so the user can retry without losing their edits. This guards against losing edits on transient disk errors such as AV sharing violations or an external editor holding the file open.
- 2026-04-17: `patchOrAppendEdits` replaces only the LAST matching key line per file, not every occurrence. `Config.loadRecursiveFiles` takes the last value when a key appears multiple times, so updating only the last occurrence preserves intentional earlier overrides (e.g. a user who had two `font-size = 12` entries deliberately — one in a theme block, one overriding — keeps the first line untouched). Pass 1 scans for last-match indices, pass 2 emits with replacements only at those indices, pass 3 appends keys that never appeared.
- 2026-04-17: Settings save short-circuits when `user_edited` bitset is empty — no-op saves don't touch the filesystem. Pressing Save without any edit on a first-run machine (no existing config file) previously created a zero-byte file that fired `error.FileIsEmpty` warnings on every subsequent load; the short-circuit prevents that and also avoids needless mtime bumps on existing files.
- 2026-04-17: `src/renderer/opengl/RenderPass.zig` MUST call `gl.viewport` after binding the pass attachment. FBO binds do not resize the viewport; on Win32 resize that leaves drawing clipped to the previous bottom-left sub-rect, which shows up as black growth bands on enlarge and stretched/distorted text on shrink.
- 2026-04-17: `Surface.writeClipboardHtml` supplies a real plain-text fallback when the core only provides `text/html`. `stripHtmlTags` walks the bytes and drops everything between `<` and `>` — best-effort, sufficient for Notepad / cmd.exe consumers. Malformed input (unclosed `<`) preserves the unmatched tail as literal text to avoid data loss. When the core supplies BOTH `text/html` and `text/plain` (the common `copy_on_select` path), the caller-supplied plain text wins and the stripper doesn't run.
- 2026-04-17: `UndoStack.clearRedo` does NOT subtract redo-entry bytes from `total_bytes` — those bytes were already deducted when the entry moved from undo → redo via `popForUndo`. `total_bytes` tracks only the undo-stack footprint for cap enforcement; double-subtraction underflows on the common "undo, then perform a new action" sequence (panic in safe builds, huge wrapped value in release).
- 2026-04-17: `settingsSaveAndReload` uses `@import("../config/key.zig").Key` to access the Config key enum — `configpkg` does not re-export `Key` at module root. The inline-for field walker in the save path is the single consumer of this import; if that helper moves to another module, relocate the import with it.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_quick_terminal.zig` geometry is allocation-free. `QuickTerminalConfig` maps directly onto the 7 `quick-terminal-*` config fields; `computeEndRect` / `computeStartRect` produce pixel rects for the slide tween, and `lerpRect` clamps `t ∈ [0,1]`. `unionMonitors` spans the virtual desktop for `screen == .all`. Config enum translation quirks: `QuickTerminalScreen.mouse` maps to `.focused`, `QuickTerminalSpaceBehavior.remain` maps to `.ignore`, `QuickTerminalKeyboardInteractivity.exclusive` maps to `.always` and `.none` maps to `.never`. The current apprt path snaps to the end rect immediately via `SetWindowPos`.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_surface_drop.zig` payload parsing is pure. `requiresQuoting` covers space, tab, `"`, `$`, backtick, `|`, `&`, `(`, `)` — the overlap between POSIX-sh and PowerShell. Single-quote, backslash, and glob chars (`*`, `?`, `~`, `#`) are deliberately excluded. `formatFilePayload` applies Shift → parent directory (via `parentDir`, which handles drive letters + mixed separators), Ctrl → suppress quoting. `extractHtmlFragment` trims to `<!--StartFragment-->` ... `<!--EndFragment-->`; malformed input returns the input unchanged. `IDropTarget` owns runtime `RegisterDragDrop` and `IDataObject::GetData` integration.
- 2026-04-17: `toggleQuickTerminal` honours `quick-terminal-keyboard-interactivity`: `on-demand` / `exclusive` call `surface.present()` (activates); `none` shows the window via `setVisible(true)` without stealing focus so users can glance at the QT as a passive log. `quick-terminal-screen = mouse` uses `GetCursorPos` + `MonitorFromPoint`, NOT `GetForegroundWindow`'s monitor — the config's documented meaning is "monitor under the cursor". Multi-monitor correctness for `.mouse` depends on this; reverting to foreground-window monitor opens the QT on the wrong screen when the pointer is parked elsewhere.
- 2026-04-17: `settingsSaveAndReload` writes `FileFormatter{ .changed = true }` — only fields differing from `Config.default()` land in `ghostty.conf`. This is load-bearing for two reasons: (1) prevents bloating the primary file to hundreds of default lines on the first click of Save; (2) future upstream default changes still take effect on fields the user didn't explicitly edit. The save-path baseline (`file_cfg` built from `default() + loadDefaultFiles`) + copy-only-edited-fields patch still applies; the formatter's `changed` flag is the second line of defence.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_tab_drag.zig` state is pure. `DragState.beginPress` / `onMouseMove` / `onMouseUp` drive the `none → maybe_tab → drag_tab` transitions; threshold is 5 px L1 distance (Manhattan, matches Windows `SM_CXDRAG`/`SM_CYDRAG` per-axis semantics and is more sensitive on diagonals than Euclidean would be — touchpad-friendly). `insertionIndexAtX` uses left-half/right-half bisection; `isNoOpDrop(source=i, insertion=i)` AND `isNoOpDrop(source=i, insertion=i+1)` both return true (drop-after-self is a no-op once the source is removed from the array). `CF_NOCTTY_TAB` payload is an `extern struct` with magic + version + PID + state_ptr; `decodePayload` rejects `error.WrongProcess` for cross-process drops — NOT a security boundary (a local attacker can spoof trivially), just correctness protection against accidental cross-install drops. OLE/COM integration must keep these invariants.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_search_bar.zig` owns per-pane search state: query (allocator-owned, freed + re-duped on `setQuery`), toggles (regex / case / word / wrap), match counts (`total` + `selected`), and a 40-ms debounce (`last_edit_ms` + `searched` flag). `shouldRunSearch(now)` returns true exactly once per debounce window after the query stabilises — the `searched` flag prevents re-firing on subsequent ticks until the next `setQuery` or `forceSearch`. `setResults(n)` primes `selected = 1` so the "1 of N" label is immediately meaningful; `setResults(0)` nulls `selected` to avoid a stale ghost. Toggles PERSIST across `close` / `open` by design — users iterating on case/word shouldn't lose progress. `downsampleMarkers` buckets matches into `max_markers` equal-height bins and emits the midpoint of each occupied bin as a normalised `[0, 1]` f32; N ≤ max returns all matches unchanged.
- 2026-04-17: `UndoStack.popForUndo` / `popForRedo` return `Allocator.Error!?*const Entry` — a borrowed pointer into the _destination_ stack (redo after undo, undo after redo), not a copy-by-value. Returning `Entry` by value aliases the snapshot's owned byte slices between the stack slot and the caller, making the first `entry.deinit` a UAF on the remaining holder. Callers must consume the pointer synchronously (before any `push` / `pop` on that stack invalidates `ArrayList` addresses) and MUST NOT call `deinit` on the pointee — the stack still owns it. On `append` failure, both stacks are rolled back to a consistent state and the allocator error is propagated; do not collapse this into "nothing to undo".
- 2026-04-17: `App.cli_config_override_path` caches the `--config-file <path>` override resolved once at `App.init` — BEFORE `run()` calls `sanitizeCurrentDirectory()`. Resolving a relative CLI path via `getCwdAlloc` at save time targets the sanitized cwd (typically `%USERPROFILE%`), which is NOT the cwd `Config.load()` used at launch. Result would be that saves land in a different file than the one CLI-masks the config at runtime. The Settings "Save" path and the Advanced "Open in editor" escape hatch both route through this cached absolute path, guaranteeing they edit the same file that `Config.load` used at launch.
- 2026-04-17: `Surface.close(process_active = true)` declines to destroy the HWND on `host.showConfirm` failure — it logs and returns without calling `DestroyWindow`. Destroying the surface unconditionally in the catch branch would silently bypass `confirm-close-surface` every time the overlay couldn't render, which is exactly the property the config is supposed to enforce. If `showConfirm` keeps failing, that's a chrome-layer bug to diagnose; the user can retry the close action once the overlay is healthy.
- 2026-04-17: `App.startup_cwd` caches the launch-time cwd (captured in `App.init` before `sanitizeCurrentDirectory()` runs). Both `settingsSaveAndReload` AND the `.reload_config` action wrap `Config.load(alloc)` in a `chdir(startup_cwd) … chdir(cwd_before)` dance so `loadCliArgs` resolves relative `--config-file` argv entries against the SAME cwd the launch used. Without this, a reload after cwd-sanitization resolves `--config-file custom.conf` to `%USERPROFILE%\custom.conf` instead of the file Config.load originally loaded from `X\custom.conf`, silently swapping the runtime config source every time the user reloads. Always restore the sanitized cwd after the reload so downstream code (surface spawn, relative-path lookups) sees the environment it expects.
- 2026-04-17: `settingsSaveAndReload` calls `makePath(dirname(target_path))` before creating the sibling temp file. `config_edit.openPath` guarantees the default-path parent exists; CLI overrides to arbitrary paths do not, so the first Save to `D:\new-project\custom.conf` would otherwise fail with `TempCreateFailed` even though the user expectation is that Save creates whatever it needs.
- 2026-04-17: `WinrtToast.showWithLaunch(title, body, severity, launch)` stamps the toast XML with a `launch="…"` attribute Windows passes verbatim to the activation handler when the toast is clicked from Action Center. `win32_toast_activation.parseLaunchArg` consumes that string from argv on cold-start activation. The base `show` forwards with `launch = null` (activation path unreachable — app just starts / foregrounds). `buildToastXml` XML-escapes the launch string so `&`-separated query params survive `IXmlDocumentIO.LoadXml` parsing.
- 2026-07-23: `patchOrAppendEdits` preserves leading indentation and whole-line comments, but must not preserve a `#` or `;` suffix after `=`. The config grammar has no inline comments; those characters are literal value data (notably in `command` and color values). Reattaching an old suffix after formatting a GUI edit silently restores value text the user replaced.
- 2026-04-17: `settingsNotifySuccessThunk` has two visible channels: (1) always tries `showHostBanner(.app, .info, "title: body")` — local chrome feedback for a user-initiated Save action, NOT gated on notification config because users expect confirmation of their own clicks; (2) conditionally fires `showDesktopNotification` gated on `app-notifications.config-reload`, or when no host banner rendered. Without channel 1, disabling config-reload notifications made settings saves completely silent.
- 2026-04-17: `App.showDesktopNotification` log-only fallback passes the COMPOSED `message` (`title: body`) to `showInfoMessage`, not raw `body`. Some callers (settings-save success) send empty body with meaningful title; using raw body produced a blank fallback entry even though the action succeeded.
- 2026-04-17: `GenericRenderer.changeConfig` propagates `window-vsync` to `api.vsync_enabled` and clears `api.swap_interval_configured` when the config toggle changes. On Win32 this is what makes runtime toggles actually land — the OpenGL backend caches the first-present swap-interval setup and skips re-application until the cache gate is cleared. Guarded by `@hasField` so the branch compiles cleanly on backends that don't expose vsync.
- 2026-04-17: `win32_paste_protection.hasShellMetachar` flags bare `$<alpha>` / `$_` (PowerShell variable expansion inside double quotes) and `%NAME%` (cmd.exe env-var expansion inside double quotes) in addition to the `;`, `|`, `&`, backtick, `$(`, `${`, `<`, `>` set. Double-quoting alone doesn't prevent interpolation in either shell — a filename like `%TEMP%.txt` dropped into cmd.exe would paste as an expanded path.
- 2026-04-17: `surfaceDropPayloadCallback` consults `win32_paste_protection.inspect()` — the FULL verdict (`control_chars`, `shell_metachar`, `mixed_content`, `contains_newline`) — before calling `completeClipboardRequest`. Core's `input.paste.isSafe` only checks newlines + bracketed-paste-end; the richer classifier catches control bytes, metachars, and embedded URLs that the core gate would pass through. Logs `verdict.severity` + `verdict.reason` so debugging-level telemetry captures why a drop was gated.
- 2026-04-17: `settingsNotifySuccessThunk` falls through to the non-modal `showDesktopNotification` fallback when `showHostBanner(.app, …)` returns false (no host surface — settings window is the only UI). Without the fallback, combining `app-notifications.config-reload = false` with a settings-only state produced a Save that succeeded silently in every observable channel.
- 2026-04-17: `win32_surface_drop.extractHtmlFragment` parses `StartFragment:` / `EndFragment:` numeric byte offsets from the CF_HTML header FIRST, then falls back to `<!--StartFragment-->` / `<!--EndFragment-->` comment markers. Numeric offsets are spec-mandatory; the comment markers are optional and absent from some drag sources (browsers, Office). Without the offset path, those sources dropped the entire CF_HTML envelope — `Version:0.9\r\n…` — into the terminal. Tests cover both paths + "offsets win on conflict" precedence.
- 2026-04-17: `win32_surface_drop.quoting_chars` additionally includes `%` (cmd.exe env-var expansion fires inside ANY quoting style) and `{` / `}` (zsh/bash brace expansion). Quoting alone can't prevent interpolation in every shell — PowerShell expands `$var` inside double quotes, cmd expands `%VAR%` even inside double quotes. Defense-in-depth: the drop callback routes the payload through `completeClipboardRequest(.paste, …, confirmed=false)`, which triggers the non-modal paste-protection overlay whenever the content contains shell metacharacters. The quoting_chars list is the first-line heuristic; user confirmation is the final gate.
- 2026-04-17: `__ghostty_encode_cwd` (PowerShell integration.ps1) uses `[uri]::EscapeDataString` PER-SEGMENT instead of `EscapeUriString` once over the whole path. EscapeUriString preserves URI separators as literals, which produces broken file:// URIs for paths with `#`, `?`, `%`, `&`, `+`, or spaces (`C:\Users\amant\project#v1` becomes `file://HOST/C:/Users/amant/project#v1` where `#` starts a URL fragment). Per-segment encoding handles UNC paths — `\\server\share\dir` emits `//server/share/encoded` from the helper, which the caller prefixes with `file:` (NOT `file://${host_name}/`) for UNC-authority URIs.
- 2026-04-17: `Host.activateTabIndex` calls `layout()` after updating `active_tab`. Layout's tab-strip pass feeds the new active tab's host-local `(left, width)` to `retargetTabUnderline`, which kicks off the 160 ms sliding-underline animation. Without the explicit layout call, the slide would only start on the next incidental layout tick (next frame's size recompute), producing a visible lag between tab-click and slide-start. Reduced-motion collapses the slide to a snap via `retargetTabUnderline`'s guard.
- 2026-04-17: `Host.rebuildPaletteList` logs breadcrumb lines (`text_len`, `cmds`, `cvals`, `ranked`) at `.debug` around the `rankedForQuery` call so palette-crash diagnostics have repro-time state.
- 2026-04-17: The `.reload_config` action handler at `win32.zig:~2456` uses the same `defer`-keyed `did_chdir_to_startup` pattern as `settingsSaveAndReload` so cwd restore runs on both the happy path AND the `Config.load` error path. Manual Ctrl+Shift+R reloads that fail on a broken include no longer pin the process to `startup_cwd`.
- 2026-04-17: Status bar height is hard-zeroed (`statusBarHeight` returns 0). User feedback: "Remove this entire bottom bar, it is far too busy. Give the user back some terminal space." The `win32_status_bar` module + its helpers stay in the live-infrastructure block for opt-in; chrome no longer reserves vertical space for the bar.
- 2026-04-17: Integrated-titlebar min/max dispatch uses explicit `SendMessageW(WM_SYSCOMMAND, SC_*)` from `WM_NCLBUTTONDOWN`, then swallows the matching mouse-up. With `WM_NCCALCSIZE` zeroing the top margin, DWM no longer owns the caption buttons and mouse-up translation can require a second click. Keep `IsZoomed` for SC_RESTORE vs SC_MAXIMIZE and keep `HTMAXBUTTON` hit testing intact for Snap Layouts.
- 2026-04-17: `win32_icons.drawMaximize` + `drawRestore` select the stock NULL brush before `Rectangle(hdc, ...)`. `setupGdi` installs a SOLID brush matching the glyph colour, so the bare `Rectangle` call FILLS the rect (producing a coloured block the user saw as a "blank button"). NULL brush draws outline only — matches Windows Terminal's glyph treatment.
- 2026-04-17: `start_search` action opens the search overlay BEFORE `setSearchText`. Reversed order let the search thread compute viewport matches against the pre-resize viewport; after the overlay shrunk the surface by ~40 px, those stale pins painted 2-3 rows below the actual match. The rendered dims_changed guard in `generic.zig:1229` clears the stale matches post-resize, but re-order ensures the first compute happens post-resize instead of relying on the guard.
- 2026-04-17: `settingsSaveAndReload` wraps the `startup_cwd` chdir in a `defer` keyed on a `did_chdir_to_startup` flag so the cwd restore runs on BOTH the happy path and the `error.ReloadFailed` path. The prior inline restore short-circuited when `Config.load` failed (broken included config file), pinning the process cwd to `startup_cwd` for the rest of the run; downstream surface spawns inherited the wrong working directory until restart.
- 2026-04-17: `stripHtmlTags` on unmatched `<` preserves the unmatched tail as literal text. The CF_UNICODETEXT fallback is best-effort plain text, and dropping the malformed remainder loses user-visible clipboard text.
- 2026-04-17: `Host.completeCommandPalette` dupes `text` AND `seed` into owned buffers BEFORE calling `setOverlayEditText(candidate)`. That call path frees `cached_overlay_edit` via `syncWindowTextUtf8CachedAfterSet → appendOwnedString`, so any read of `text` / `seed` after it is a use-after-free — `text` is a slice into `cached_overlay_edit` (from `overlayEditText`), and `seed` either aliases `text` or borrows from the same cache. This is the task-#44 root cause: "palette crashes after a few keystrokes" fired on type → Tab because `appendOwnedString(seed)` at the end of the function read the just-freed memory. NEVER retain a slice returned by `overlayEditText` across a call that might update the overlay EDIT — always dupe.
- 2026-04-17: Tab close-fade uses TWO `CloseState` slots on `Host` — `tab_close_hover_*` drives the incoming-target fade-in (0 → 1); `tab_close_prev_*` drives the departing tab's fade-out (1 → 0) on adjacent-tab transitions. Without the second slot, rapidly moving the mouse from tab A to tab B would snap A's X to 0 the moment the mouse enters B instead of cross-fading them. Both slots are tick-ed + invalidated independently in `tickTweens`. A single extra slot is enough — at 120 ms per fade the user can't queue a third parallel animation within the heartbeat.
- 2026-04-17: Tab close-button hover fade is the first visible consumer of `win32_tab_visual.CloseState`. Wired through `Host.setHoveredButton` → `addTween(dummy, close_fade_ms)` → `tickTweens` explicitly invalidates the fading tab button HWND (parent-HWND `InvalidateRect` with flag 0 does NOT cascade to child buttons). `drawButton` reads `CloseState.alphaAt(GetTickCount64())` and alpha-pre-composites the close-glyph colour against the tab bg via `blendColorRGB` — GDI `DrawTextW` has no alpha channel, so the blend must happen on the colour before SetTextColor. Active tabs always paint the X at full alpha (permanent affordance); non-hovered + non-active tabs skip the glyph entirely when alpha ≤ 0.01.
- 2026-04-17: `blendColorRGB(a, b, alpha)` linearly interpolates two Windows COLORREFs (`0x00BBGGRR`) by `alpha` ∈ [0, 1]. Clamps alpha, extracts channels, lerps, reassembles. Used for fade effects on GDI paint paths that can't use alpha blending directly (DrawTextW, FillRect). Do NOT pass hex literals that include the high byte — Windows COLORREF is 24-bit, the high byte is reserved and must be zero.
- 2026-04-17: Palette paint + dispatch paths defensively bounds-check `palette_list_ranked[i]` against the fixed stack buffer length (`256`), NOT just against `palette_list_ranked_count`. `rankedForQuery` is contracted to respect the buffer length, but a regression in that contract would turn every paint / scroll / invoke into OOB UB. Also bounds-check `cmd_index` against BOTH `snap.commands.len` AND `snap.cvals.len` — the two arrays are parallel but a user-added `command-palette-entry` could desync them if parsing was partial.
- 2026-04-17: `win32_surface_drop.requiresQuoting` must include `;` because it is a statement separator in PowerShell and POSIX shells; over-quoting is safer than command injection. Locked by `test "requiresQuoting: semicolon (command separator)"`.
- 2026-04-17: `win32_surface_drop_target.probeFormats` falls back to per-format `QueryGetData` probes when `EnumFormatEtc` is absent or returns `E_NOTIMPL`. Some `IDataObject` providers (older browsers, limited test shells) don't implement enumeration even though `GetData` works correctly. Without the fallback, otherwise-valid drops get rejected as "no accepted formats". `queryGetDataForFormat` submits a `FORMATETC{cf, DVASPECT_CONTENT, TYMED_HGLOBAL}` and checks the S_OK result — doesn't allocate or touch the data.
- 2026-04-17: `Host.rebuildPaletteList` must NOT free the slice `overlayEditText` returns. `overlayEditText` returns either a `""` literal or a borrowed view of `Host.cached_overlay_edit` — the Host owns the cache and frees it in `Host.deinit`. A prior defer-free both (a) freed a string literal (UB) and (b) left `cached_overlay_edit` pointing at freed memory, so every subsequent palette read returned a dangling pointer. Callers read, never own.
- 2026-04-17: Surface drag-drop payloads route through `core_surface.completeClipboardRequest(.paste, payload_z, false)` — NEVER through `core_surface.textCallback`. `textCallback` hard-codes `allow_unsafe=true` (it's the terminal-typed path where newlines ARE user intent), which silently defeats `clipboard-paste-protection` for dropped content. On `error.UnsafePaste` / `UnauthorizedPaste`, or a non-`.safe` `win32_paste_protection.inspect` verdict, route through `requestPasteConfirm` / `HostOverlayMode.confirm` and re-submit asynchronously from `surfaceConfirmPasteAccept`.
- 2026-04-17: `App.toggleQuickTerminal` calls `surface.setVisible(false)` BEFORE `ShowWindow(SW_HIDE)` on hide, and `surface.setVisible(true)` AFTER `ShowWindow(SW_SHOW)` on show — hiding the top-level HWND alone leaves `Surface.window_visible = true`, so the renderer thread keeps waking and `core_surface.occlusionCallback` never fires. Per the 2026-04-14 invariant that's a crash in `nvoglv64!DrvPresentBuffers`. The dual-call dance keeps the child Surface and the top-level HWND in sync.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_toast_activation.zig` parses `wgh://activate?surface=…&tab=…&window=…&action=…` launch strings emitted from the WinRT toast XML's `<toast launch="…">` attribute. Pure stdlib, no Win32 deps. Cold-start, forwarded-argv, and warm `IToastNotification.Activated` paths all resolve through `App.handleToastActivation`, preferring surface id, then tab/window, then the primary surface. Forward-compat: unknown query keys and unknown `action` values are ignored so newer toasts don't crash older builds.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_surface_drop_target.zig` owns a minimal `IDropTarget` COM implementation that translates Explorer / browser drops into typed PTY input. Registered per-surface via `RegisterDragDrop` in `Surface.init` (after the HWND exists), revoked in `Surface.destroy` BEFORE the HWND is destroyed (Ole would otherwise hold a dangling HWND reference for the rest of the process). Storage is INLINE on the Surface struct (never-moved heap-allocated owner), so the vtbl-parent-pointer trick in `DropTarget.fromObj` stays stable. The payload callback (`surfaceDropPayloadCallback`) routes through `core_surface.completeClipboardRequest(.paste, payload, false)`, so `clipboard-paste-protection` gates drops; on `UnsafePaste` / `UnauthorizedPaste`, queue `Surface.requestPasteConfirm` and resubmit with `confirmed=true`. STA apartment from `App.init`'s `CoInitializeEx` is a prerequisite; COM callbacks arrive only on the UI thread.
- 2026-04-17: `hostButtonProc` handles keyboard events for the overlay accept/cancel pair: `VK_ESCAPE` cancels (confirm mode) / dismisses (generic overlay); `VK_TAB` / `VK_LEFT` / `VK_RIGHT` cycle focus between the two buttons; `VK_RETURN` / `VK_SPACE` invoke the focused button's action. Without this path, confirm-mode Esc was inert because focus sits on accept_hwnd (the EDIT control is hidden) and the only Esc handler lived on `overlayEditProc`. Keyboard cancellation is non-negotiable for modal-ish overlays — mouse-only dismiss is an accessibility regression.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_powershell_install.zig` is wired live in `App.init`: `resolveInstallPath` drops to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\noctty\shell-integration\powershell\integration.ps1`, `installIfStale` writes atomically (temp file + rename) iff the on-disk SHA-256 differs from the embedded blob, and `buildInjectedArgv` now owns the interactive PowerShell argv wrapper logic. Failure is silent (log-warn + continue) — the installed copy is the manual `$PROFILE` fallback, so a failed install never blocks a terminal. Embedded via `@embedFile`, so `zig build` packaging captures the current `integration.ps1` — changes ship in the same binary.
- 2026-04-17: `src/apprt/win32_toast_winrt.zig` owns runtime-loaded `ToastNotificationManager` bindings. `App.winrt_toast` is an optional; init failure is non-fatal (log-warn + stay null, fallback to host banner/log). `showDesktopNotification` tries the WinRT path FIRST and **returns on success** — showing the banner AND the Action Center toast on the same event double-notifies the user (same content in two places at once). Only if `show` errors (Focus Assist suppressed the toast, user disabled notifications, XML rejected, etc.) does the banner fallback fire. WinRT `init` MUST run after `setProcessAumid` because `CreateToastNotifierWithId` binds the notifier to whatever AUMID the process has at call time. `deinit` must balance every `RoInitialize` result of `S_OK` or `S_FALSE` with `RoUninitialize`; do not call it for `RPC_E_CHANGED_MODE`. All combase.dll functions are loaded via `GetProcAddress` — never link directly — so corporate-lockdown machines without combase just take the fallback path.
- 2026-04-17: Palette EN_CHANGE re-entry guard. Programmatic `SetWindowTextW` on the overlay EDIT HWND synchronously fires `EN_CHANGE` on the same thread, which previously (a) nulled `cached_overlay_edit`, (b) re-ran `syncCommandPaletteBanner` / `syncOverlay*` mid-write, and (c) raced borrows of the cache across the outer caller's follow-up sync. `Host.suppress_edit_events: bool` is the guard flag; `Host.setOverlayEditText(value)` is the sole wrapper around `syncWindowTextUtf8CachedAfterSet` for the edit HWND — it sets the flag before `SetWindowTextW`, clears it after, and the EN_CHANGE handler early-returns when the flag is true. Mirrors the precedent in `win32_settings.zig` (AGENTS.md:75). All four programmatic edit writes (`stepProfileSelection`, `showOverlay`, `completeCommandPalette`, `stepTabOverviewSelection`) route through the wrapper. Adding a fifth programmatic write must use `setOverlayEditText`, not raw `syncWindowTextUtf8CachedAfterSet` against `&cached_overlay_edit`, or the crash returns. Locked by `test "win32 palette EN_CHANGE re-entry guard"`.
- 2026-04-17: `appendOwnedString` allocates the replacement BEFORE freeing the old slot. Reversed from the prior order so (a) an OOM leaves `*target` holding a still-valid previous allocation instead of a dangling freed pointer, and (b) a caller passing a `value` that aliases the current slot is copied to fresh storage before the free, closing a latent UAF class. All live callers are null-safe re: the old order; the new order is strictly more robust. Locked by `test "win32 appendOwnedString leaves target untouched on OOM"`.
- 2026-04-17: Search-highlight drift guard. In `src/renderer/generic.zig`'s `updateFrame`, snapshot `terminal_state.rows` / `cols` BEFORE `terminal_state.update`, and on dim change (a) deinit + null `search_matches` and `search_selected_match`, (b) raise `search_matches_dirty` so the highlight apply-loop erases old paint, (c) raise `state.terminal.flags.search_viewport_dirty` so the search thread recomputes against the fresh viewport. Without this, a resize (overlay open/close, window resize, font-size change) leaves the renderer painting pre-resize pins against post-resize `row_pins` — the symptom "yellow highlight rect 2-3 rows below the actual match" matches `ceil(host_overlay_height / cell_height)`. The `renderer.Message.SearchMatches` comment at `generic.zig:135-136` explicitly notes pins may be invalid and "must be validated prior to use"; the dim-change guard is that validation. Future improvement: `highlight.Flattened.Chunk.serial` is stored but unchecked — on-reuse validation would also catch node-memory recycling UAF.
- 2026-04-17: Focused-tab underline is painted as a single parent-HWND rect animated via `Host.tab_underline: win32_tab_visual.UnderlineState`. `retargetTabUnderline(left, width)` is called from `layoutChromeForRect` once per layout, snapping on the first retarget (slide_started_ms == 0, no slide-in-from-origin on window open) and under reduced-motion / HC, otherwise sliding over `underline_slide_ms` (160 ms). The slide runs on the existing 16 ms tween heartbeat via a dummy `addTween`; no second timer. `paintChrome` draws the interpolated rect in the 2 px gap between the child tab buttons (`button_y = scaled(3)`, `button_height = tab_h - scaled(6)`, so buttons occupy y∈[3, tab_h-3)) and the 1 px chrome bottom border. Painting on the parent (not each child button) is load-bearing: only a single-rect paint can slide smoothly across child-HWND boundaries; per-button underline is a hard cut. The per-button underline in `drawButton` is removed. Insets 3 px on each horizontal edge per WT reference (docs/wt-chrome-reference.md §1, MUX `TabViewItemBorderThickness: 1,1,1,0` equivalent at our scale).
- 2026-04-17: `App.showInfoMessage` is a log-only tertiary fallback, not a modal. Primary notification path: WinRT Action Center toast (via `App.winrt_toast`); secondary: host banner (`showHostBanner`); tertiary: `std.log.info`. Reached only when there is no visible target (no host) and WinRT init/show failed (Focus Assist suppression, disabled app notifications, corporate lockdown without combase). Do not reintroduce modal info popups for notification fallback.
- 2026-04-17: Integrated titlebar Phase A (Win11 build ≥ 22000, gated on `App.use_integrated_titlebar`). `Host.handleNcCalcSize` calls DefWindowProcW to get the default frame, then resets `rgrc[0].top` to include the caption row; maximized state adds `size_frame_y + padded_border` for Win11's invisible resize margin. `Host.handleNcHitTest` dispatches via `win32_nc_layout.hitTest` — keep HTCLOSE/HTMAXBUTTON/HTMINBUTTON pixel-exact so Snap Layouts fires on maxbutton hover. `Host.handleNcMouseMove` updates `caption_hover`, arms `TrackMouseEvent(TME_LEAVE | TME_NONCLIENT)`, and repaints only the top chrome; `WM_SIZE SIZE_MINIMIZED/SIZE_MAXIMIZED` clears caption hover/pressed state because Win32 can cancel NC tracking silently. Min/max dispatch is explicit from `WM_NCLBUTTONDOWN`; close dispatch remains on mouse-up. `tabBarHeight()` returns `host_tab_height_integrated` (40 px) when integrated. `layoutChromeForRect` reserves `3 * host_caption_button_w` px on the right, and `paintCaptionButtons` uses Segoe Fluent/MDL2 glyphs with 1 px vector fallback.
- 2026-04-17: `App.use_integrated_titlebar` must gate on BOTH Win11 build support and `config."window-show-tab-bar" != .never`. Enabling integrated NC hit-testing while the tab/caption row is hidden leaves invisible custom chrome active. On config reload, recompute the gate and force `SWP_FRAMECHANGED` + relayout on every host when it flips so the frame doesn't stay stale.
- 2026-04-17: Host-side Win11 chrome consumers (`WM_NCCALCSIZE` / `WM_NCHITTEST` / caption-button paint / WM_NCLBUTTONUP dispatch) must gate on the host actually using an integrated caption row, not only `App.use_integrated_titlebar`. The app flag is the build/config floor; host logic must also respect real row visibility so future tab-bar visibility changes don't leave custom NC behavior active against hidden chrome.
- 2026-04-17: On Win32 frame-mode flips (stock caption <-> integrated titlebar), clear `Host.caption_hover` and `caption_track_armed` during the reconfigure path. `TrackMouseEvent(TME_NONCLIENT)` can be canceled by the frame transition without delivering `WM_NCMOUSELEAVE`; if those fields survive, re-enabling integrated chrome can repaint a stale hovered caption button before the mouse moves again.
- 2026-04-17: Custom Win32 caption paths should pass `WM_NCHITTEST` and `WM_NCMOUSELEAVE` through `DwmDefWindowProc` first, and let `WM_NCLBUTTONUP` try DWM before falling back to explicit `WM_SYSCOMMAND`. This preserves native caption-button handling where DWM still owns it, while keeping the explicit min/max/restore fallback for builds that stop translating HT* after custom `WM_NCCALCSIZE`.
- 2026-04-17: `confirmClipboard*` async migration — **zero live `MessageBoxW` call sites in the apprt**. Surface now owns a `pending_clipboard_op: ?PendingClipboardOp` tagged union carrying deep-copied payload bytes across a `HostOverlayMode.confirm` roundtrip. `Surface.requestPasteConfirm(request, data)` dupes `data` (`[:0]u8`) and parks a `.paste` op; `Surface.requestWriteConfirm(clipboard, contents)` dupes each `ClipboardContent`'s mime + data and parks a `.write` op. `surfaceConfirmPasteAccept` / `surfaceConfirmWriteAccept` callbacks run the action on accept (snapshot payload + clear pending BEFORE dispatching, mirroring AGENTS.md:89's "no `self` access after dispatch" rule); `surfaceConfirmPasteCancel` / `surfaceConfirmWriteCancel` free the payload on cancel. `Surface.destroy` drains any pending op so close-while-pending can't leak. Former `confirmClipboardRead/Write` call sites (`surfaceDropPayloadCallback` paste-protection branch + UnsafePaste fallback, `Surface.clipboardRequest` UnsafePaste fallback, `Surface.setClipboard` confirm=true branch) now route through the async helpers; the synchronous `confirmMessageBox` wrapper + `clipboard_read_title/message` + `clipboard_write_title/message` UTF-16 literals are deleted. `Surface.setClipboard`'s receiver changed from `*const Surface` → `*Surface` so queuing can mutate Surface; core-side signature is unchanged (Surface.zig already holds `rt_surface: *apprt.runtime.Surface`). The `MessageBoxW` extern is kept as a dormant binding for a pre-init fatal-error dialog if one is added.
- 2026-04-17: Clipboard confirm hardening. **A.** `Surface.clipboardRequest` collapsed the outer `defer if (text)` + inner `.osc_52_read` fallback `dupeZ("")` into a single `defer alloc.free(str)` because the previous structure leaked the fallback allocation on the empty-clipboard osc-52 path (never owned by `text`). **B.** `Surface.requestPasteConfirm` and `requestWriteConfirm` moved the `pending_clipboard_op` commit from BEFORE `showConfirm` to AFTER it, eliminating a double-`errdefer` that would free the payload twice on `showConfirm` failure. Safe because `showConfirm` stores callbacks but does NOT invoke them synchronously (user interaction dispatches via `hostWindowProc` WM_COMMAND on a later pump tick). **C.** `surfaceConfirmPasteAccept` now snapshots `surface.app.core_app.alloc` into a local `alloc` value BEFORE dispatching `completeClipboardRequest`. If the core action synchronously destroys the Surface (for example `close_surface`), the `defer alloc.free(pending.data)` still runs against a valid allocator (std.mem.Allocator is a value; the ctx it wraps is app-scoped and outlives the surface). `surfaceConfirmWriteAccept` already captured alloc before dispatch. **D.** Added `Host.cancelConfirmIfOwnedBy(ud: *anyopaque)` — hides + deinits the active confirm overlay when its userdata equals `ud` WITHOUT dispatching on_cancel (the Surface is tearing down, there's nothing to roll back). Called from `Surface.destroy` before the `pending_clipboard_op` drain so a later user click on a stale overlay can't fire `surfaceConfirmPasteAccept/Cancel(&freed_surface)` → UAF.
- 2026-04-17: Clipboard confirm write-allocation guard. `requestWriteConfirm`'s dup loop uses a per-iteration `errdefer alloc.free(mime_copy)` so a partial iteration (mime dup succeeds, subsequent data dup fails) can't leak the successful mime dup. The outer errdefer still frees `[0..filled)` + the backing slice for the showConfirm-failure case. Atomic commit: both dupes succeed THEN `owned[filled] = ...` + `filled += 1`, so the per-iter errdefer goes out of scope without firing on the success path. `invokeConfirmAccept` / `invokeConfirmCancel` both call `hideOverlay` + `layout` FIRST and dispatch the callback LAST, so a destructive accept (close_surface / close_tab) can free the Host without UAFing the `self.*` accesses in `hideOverlay`.
- 2026-04-18: In `tabButtonProc`, snapshot `tab_drag_index` / `tab_drag_active` BEFORE `ReleaseCapture()`. Win32 fires `WM_CAPTURECHANGED` synchronously during `ReleaseCapture`, and our handler clears those fields; reading them afterward swallows normal single-click tab activation and leaves only later button-default behavior.
- 2026-04-18: In `tabButtonProc`, once the custom tab path calls `SetCapture` on `WM_LBUTTONDOWN`, return `0` instead of falling through to the previous BUTTON wndproc. Letting the default owner-draw button proc run after our capture setup can desync the tab-drag state before `WM_LBUTTONUP`, making single-click activation flaky even after the `ReleaseCapture()` snapshot fix.
- 2026-04-18: Raw `zig test` on leaf modules in this repo is often misleading because the real build graph injects named modules/resources (`build_options`, `help_strings`, `terminal_options`, pkg deps, embedded assets). For isolated Zig-unit work, use a temporary package-root harness for pure helpers; for modules that reach into config/font/terminal build plumbing, fall back to `zig build test -Dtest-filter=...` or expect stub-module churn.
- 2026-04-18: PowerShell top-level CLI accepts some long-option prefixes (`-Co`/`-Com` → `-Command`, `-NoE`/`-NoEx` → `-NoExit`) but rejects ambiguous `No*` shorthands like `-No` and `-Non`. Shell-integration argv parsing must not treat every long-name prefix as valid or it will mis-dedupe `-NoExit` / misclassify `-NonInteractive`.
- 2026-04-18: In this Windows-only fork, the broadest local suite path is `zig build test -Demit-test-exe=true`; bare `zig build test` intentionally errors unless you pass `-Dtest-filter=<name>` or `-Demit-test-exe=true`.
- 2026-04-18: Overlay scrollbar child HWNDs must use `WS_EX_LAYERED` with BOTH `LWA_ALPHA` and `LWA_COLORKEY`; global alpha alone tints the entire hover strip over the GL child instead of leaving only the painted track/thumb visible.
- 2026-04-18: In Win32 `WM_COMMAND`, `lParam == 0` is valid for menu/accelerator dispatch. Never `@ptrFromInt(lParam)` unconditionally there; decode to `?HWND` first or command actions like search toggle can panic with "cast causes pointer to be null".
- 2026-04-18: In `PageList.resizeWithoutReflow`, row-growth paths intentionally make `self.rows` temporarily larger than `total_rows` before repeated `grow()` calls complete. Under slow runtime safety, pause integrity checks around those loops or `grow()`'s deferred `assertIntegrity()` will panic during maximize/live resize with active output.
- 2026-04-19: In Win32 docked search chrome, do not parent-paint grouped trays behind owner-draw child buttons. Hidden or resized child placements can keep stale rects and leave ghost boxes on resize; group with spacing, not background trays.
- 2026-04-19: In ad-hoc PowerShell launch probes, do not pass single-quoted env paths like `'$env:WINDIR\\System32\\cmd.exe'` into `Start-Process -ArgumentList`; they stay literal and create false Win32 IO-startup failures. Resolve to an absolute path first.
- 2026-04-19: Win32 docked-search edit changes must not call `host.layout()` on every `EN_CHANGE`; the only query-driven geometry toggle is the empty/non-empty results chip, so unconditional relayout just churns `invalidateHostedChildPaint` and synchronous child repaints on each keystroke.
- 2026-04-19: `invalidateHostedChildPaint` only needs to queue child `WM_PAINT` after repaint-suppressed `SetWindowPos`; forcing `UpdateWindow` there turns every layout/search resize into synchronous owner-draw churn with no correctness benefit.
- 2026-04-19: If Win32 docked-search stops clearing core search on every non-empty edit, pending debounce state must ignore incoming core `search_total` / `search_selected` / `search_match_rows` updates or stale results from the previous query overwrite the bar's `"Searching"` state and scrollbar markers before the rerun starts.
- 2026-04-19: `buildHostAwareBaseTitle` only depends on effective surface title + host tab status. Search state (`search_needle`, totals, selection, markers) is not part of the host title contract, so search callbacks must not route through `refreshWindowTitle()` unless the title builder changes too.
- 2026-04-19: `Host.tickTweens()` only drives tab-strip chrome today (`tab_underline` + tab close-hover fades). Full-window `InvalidateRect(hwnd, null, 0)` there repaints the whole host every 16 ms for a top-bar-only animation; use top-chrome repaint plus per-button invalidation.
- 2026-04-19: `Surface.windowSizeChanged()` should reuse `queuePaintRequest(false)` instead of raw `InvalidateRect`. Repeated `WM_SIZE` during Win32 resize already has a shared `paint_pending` gate there; bypassing it posts redundant child repaints for the same pending frame.
- 2026-04-19: Integrated-titlebar caption hover state (`caption_hover`) only repaints the top row. Routing `WM_NCMOUSEMOVE` / `WM_NCMOUSELEAVE` hover churn through full `invalidateChrome()` repaints bottom/status/search-frame regions for no visible change; use `repaintTopChrome()` at those callers instead.
- 2026-04-19: `swapTabs()` already drives `layout()`, and real reorder visual changes come from tab-button rect/visibility deltas there. A second generic `invalidateChrome()` after layout is redundant repaint churn on the tab-move path.
- 2026-04-19: `Surface.focusChanged()` only needs host chrome repaint for split-pane focus borders. On single-pane tabs, routing every focus flip through `invalidateChrome()` repaints host chrome for no visible change; gate it on `leafCount() > 1`.
- 2026-04-19: Split-pane focus changes repaint only the host-painted content lane (pane-divider gaps and focused-pane border). Invalidating top chrome too is avoidable overpaint; use a content-only host repaint helper there.
- 2026-04-19: Activation fast paths must key off real HWND focus/visibility state, not just `host.active_tab` or `tab.focused`. Some callers pre-set those fields before `activateSurface`, so a naïve “already active” shortcut can silently skip real tab-switch or split-focus layout work.
- 2026-04-19: Profile-overlay chrome is top-row-only when the Win32 status bar is disabled. Routing those updates through full `invalidateChromeText()` repaints bottom/status regions for no visible change; use top-only text invalidation there and reserve full invalidation for any future status-only profile chrome.
- 2026-04-19: `Host.refreshChrome()` only has activation/init callers in this fork, and `layout()` already invalidates content/search-frame regions. With the Win32 status bar disabled, its text invalidation should repaint top chrome only; reserve full chrome-text invalidation for any future visible status bar.
- 2026-04-19: Inspector-toggle text invalidation follows the same rule: when `statusBarHeight() == 0`, `repaintTopChrome()` already covers the tab row, overlay row, and inspector panel because `contentRect().top` includes the inspector offset. Full `invalidateChromeText()` there is avoidable overpaint.
- 2026-04-19: `App.windowDestroyed()` already runs `activateSurface(...)` and `host.layout()` on the surviving host. The trailing `InvalidateRect(hwnd, null, 0)` was redundant full-host overpaint on tab/pane close; keep the relayout, drop the extra invalidate.
- 2026-04-19: `WM_DPICHANGED` already runs `recreateChromeFont()` and `layout()`, which cover child-font updates and content/search-frame invalidation. The remaining host repaint should follow the same `chromeTextNeedsFullInvalidation(statusBarHeight())` rule; unconditional `invalidateChrome()` there is avoidable overpaint when the status bar is disabled.
- 2026-04-19: `App.setUpdateNotice()` can replay the same version/URL payload across repeated update checks. Without a same-value fast path it frees/rebuilds the notice and invalidates top chrome on every host for no visible change.
- 2026-04-19: Quick-slot pin/clear actions fan out through `reapplyLauncherProfilePreferences()` and `refreshProfileChrome()` on every host. Guard the no-op cases first: replaying the same key into the same slot, or clearing when all slots are already null, is pure global churn.
- 2026-04-19: `setLauncherProfileTarget()` is another profile-chrome replay trap: when the target enum is unchanged, refreshing profile chrome is pure no-op repaint churn.
- 2026-04-19: `setSelectedProfileIndex()` can be a pure replay too. If the clamped index is unchanged, no quick-slot focus is active, and both selected-profile keys already match, rewriting the owned strings and clearing focus again is pure churn.
- 2026-04-19: Once `setSelectedProfileIndex()` can report “no effective selection change”, its callers must respect that and skip follow-on `refreshProfileChrome()` replays. Otherwise the internal fast path still leaves top-chrome invalidation churn in cycle/step/quick-open flows.
- 2026-04-19: Command-palette completion can also replay identical state. Even when `EM_SETSEL` still needs to run for the edit control, `overlay_completion_seed` and `overlay_completion_value` should not be freed and re-duped if they already match the same completion.
- 2026-04-19: `appendOwnedString()` itself can cheaply no-op on exact same-value writes. Without that guard, every caller that misses a local equality check still pays alloc/free churn, so broad cache-audit work should prefer fixing the shared helper once and validating the affected slices.
- 2026-04-18: On Windows in this repo, run `zig build*` / `zig build test*` through `scripts/dev-windows.cmd` (or an equivalent VS-dev + Zig 0.15.2 shell). The ambient Codex shell can miss the intended native toolchain setup and mislead with generic Zig/native dependency failures that disappear in the bootstrapped environment.
- 2026-04-30: Cross-thread ChatGPT URLs can stall behind Cloudflare verify-human; use `codex exec resume <thread-id> <prompt>` for coordination, and do not discard a completed streamed answer just because Codex ends with a trailing `thread ... not found` rollout-record error.
